# Sync sessions — 2026-11

Rows moved verbatim from `docs/SYNC_STATUS.md` (2026-11-08, DOC1, the frontmatter series documented once): the landed
frontmatter rows of its "Silent-loss inventory" — the batch rows 77d-108b, the observer's families (S2b), the Z rows
Z1-Z14, the bundle rows int117a-int145b, the censuses and measurements that retired. A row whose work landed with a live
residual left a one-line row in SYNC_STATUS holding the residual (its witnesses, REDs, OD numbers) and pointing here.
Also the fixed frontmatter rows of `perfect_kernel/OPEN_ROWS.md` (its fidelity audit round 2 table, one RED-repro
item and the sig-alternate rehearsals' row), each under where it was; `parity/DEFERRED_FAMILIES.md` held none fixed. Also `perfect_kernel/KERNEL_CAPABILITIES.md`'s
K23 design record, superseded by the plan (2026-11-08, DOC1's fix round). The live plan is
[`../parity/FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md`](../parity/FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md).

| — | **Frontmatter architecture: the audit's plan** (`archive/FRONTMATTER_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md`; the user's principle 2026-10-28: bindings only through the Frontmatter API, general reorganizing abstractions at every stage, special cases local in bindings or behind API opt-in data): the pipeline is Perl's (queue → digest → place) and the Rust-only API extensions are opt-in data (`owner=sequence`, `_share`, `\lx@title@inst`); ranked work — (1) the observer policy table keyed on binding file names in generic code (`observer/policy.rs:98-260`, `written: true` for revtex/ams) becomes binding-declared conventions; (2) one kill switch per general reorganizer (86g cover order, #262 placement, #246 wrap, `distribute_upfront_contacts`, `respell_emails`, K11 reroute, deposit keep), defaults unchanged, for exact A/B attribution; (3) an author-convention registry merging the ~7 drifted vocabulary lists in `author_block/` (llncs `\inst`/`\fnmsep`, ACM `\alignauthor`, IJCAI `\affiliations`, IEEE `\IEEEauthorrefmark`, `\thanksref`, `\altaffilmark`), bindings declaring their entries; (4) 86g: a sentence-length `\noindent` paragraph is not cover unless deposit-stamped (about 39 of the 295 covers rest on `ltx_noindent` alone; a CONFIRMED body-prose probe in the audit); (5) contrib bindings building `<ltx:classification>`/`<ltx:abstract>`/contacts in place (siamart, interact, jmlr, jmlr2e, ieeeojcsys, ieeetaes, agujournal2019, optica_article, dmtcs_episciences; svmult `\lx@sv@chapauthor`) go through the API; (6) the IJCAI sectioned split becomes a generic `\lx@add@authors@sectioned`; (7) a shared `frontmatter::tokens` toolkit for the six contrib bindings that re-implement the reader's primitives (ptephy, osa_journal, epl, eptcs, aistats, interspeech), as each is touched; (8) record `distribute_upfront_contacts`' grid as charter §4's list evidence or gate it; (9) one ownership rule with one home — DECIDED (user 2026-10-28: expand the home that started as the general layer): the observer (OD #837, FM S2/K23, "a single resolver" over what TeX prints) is the single home; no new ownership rule goes into `author_block/`, `shared_row` or the insert passes; the old reader keeps what feeds the events and retires per the S2 inventory; 85a's and 85b's rules are ported into the resolver when `article` and llncs reach S2c; item (1) — binding-declared conventions instead of the name-keyed policy table — comes first; **item (1) DONE (87a)**; item (7) WITHDRAWN 2026-10-29 by the digested-representation refactor (next row): each binding declares its conventions at load (`declare_frontmatter_conventions`, its `FRONTMATTER_CONVENTIONS`), the observer reads them from the state, `KERNEL` for a family that declares none; the table is gone (OD #837 "The conventions' home"; a pure refactor: census records and A/B unchanged; guard `perfect_kernel_batch87a::*`)  **Item 9 DECIDED by the user 2026-11-01 and made the frontmatter priority:** "keep evolving our design of the Frontmatter API until it can accommodate all variations used in arXiv. Prioritize refactoring to a better model of a single ownership engine, unified machinery and API, unified placement order inside the API" — one ownership engine (reader + insert-time passes and the observer converge), every binding's placement through the shared machinery (no binding-private splits: llncs, sv_support, inst_support, aa, ptephy, webofc), one placement order inside the API (marks/labels → the name a contact spells → the class's declared convention for unmarked lines → the block), bindings declaring conventions only (the ~95 per-binding `annotate=` modes: run 42, new 35, all 16, last 1, N 1). Failures of the old design that motivated it: rev97cd C1 (sv_support's split put an institute's e-mail on every author: 2101.04527, 2206.03859, 2404.15833) and F1 (aastex `annotate=run` overriding marks: 1810.01941 39 → 364 affiliations). Design study done: the plan is `parity/FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md` (the observer emits owners at the flush as Perl's own `label=`/`annotations=` keys instead of patching the old reader's DOM; Stages 0-7; rulings R1-R9); its stages are the batches after int126a. | OPEN (plan) |

| — | **Frontmatter notes read as the class prints them; a note with no ink is none (92e, 92g; user rulings 2026-10-30, FIDELITY_CHARTER §2, §4)** (OD #915, #916, #921): fixed by `name=` where the class prints another label — aa/svjour `\offprints` "Send offprint requests to:", jheppub `\arxivnumber` "ArXiv ePrint:", acmart `\acmISBN` "ACM ISBN", iopart `\submitto`/`\submitted` "Submitted to:", AASTeX `\submitjournal` "Submitted to", IEEEoj/ieeetj `\doiinfo` and old LIPIcs `\DOI` "Digital Object Identifier"; a note is dropped only when it provably prints nothing — every item known blank (`leaves_no_ink`: white space, glue, kerns, breaks, struts, phantoms, an unframed box or a constructor whose literal template prints only its arguments, over blank arguments) —, emptied by us (`\offprints{\email{…}}`: astro-ph0405280, astro-ph0608669) or by the author (`\thanks{}`, `\externaleditor{~}`: 2004.01582, 2401.15379, 2609.00671); any doubt is ink (a constructor's own text, `\openone`, `\dots`, `\LaTeX`, `$\vdots$`; an element of its own; a closure or a construction hook, `\emph{}`, `\url{}`; a sized rule, a frame, a leader, an error marker; a background colour, `\colorbox{red}{}`), and the observer drops the same notes on the same input (`ruling4:inkless`; a note's text is no part of the block's empty-mark filter). | — | **106t: the title-page check's second source — what TeX makes of what the document gave its frontmatter commands (Bundle 4; OD #953 "106t")** — DONE (r2, rule (A): digest, never read tokens): a check, the XML the base's. A class binding that writes its own frontmatter has no print run, and a binding's command may drop what pdflatex prints. The calls of LaTeXML's macros read from the document's own text (`Expandable::watched_call` → `state::note_document_call`, each argument with whether the body names it) inside which the frontmatter API queues an entry (`title_page_check::note_frontmatter_entry`) are its frontmatter commands; wanted beside the print, all digested: the entry's own digested content (`end_frontmatter_entry_digestion`, `ink_word_text`), and, digested once speculatively (sect05.rs `speculative_word_run`), a required argument the command's body never names (quantumarticle `\ead`, KPE #1208), an internal command's optional one (`\begin{abstract}[Summary]`, KPE #1209) and the document's text a binding-made macro drops while the entry digests (`state::note_dropped_argument`, filtered to what the document wrote: IEEEtran's `\lx@IEEE@membership@drop`, jmlr's `\titletag`). A binding's by-design drop is declared on its own definition, `DefMacro!(…, drops => [n])` (`Expandable::dropped_by_design`, the expansion unchanged), only where the real file prints nothing of the argument or prints it as page furniture: hyperref `\texorpdfstring` #2 (2306.06741, 2306.06006), dmtcs `\publicationdetails` #5, lipics `\volumeinfo`, lmcs `\lmcsheading`, aomart `\subject` #2, midl `\orcid`, cas `\corref`, icml/OmniBus `\comment` (main-session ruling (b)). r1's 42 token-reading false alarms pass unchanged; two guards re-pinned to true losses pdflatex prints (`batch86i` amsart `[Résumé]`, `batch86d` the pinned residual's IEEE grade). **Coverage** (98 lost-words papers): 24 → 28 (26 all, 2 some: 2209.07123, 2104.14704, 1511.03982, 2211.07413); r1's token reading named 4 more that digestion cannot (2110.09105 aa `\email jose…` an undelimited argument; 1107.2462, 2101.10506, 2203.00070 raw classes with no kept print run — 106s's reach). **False alarms:** 0 over a 500-paper bound-class control from `popint125a_s10k` and 500 random. (1) `NameKeys::of`, the one place every spelling site reads, keys a name by its base letters and by `resolver::transliteration` (ä ae, ö oe, ü ue, ß ss, å aa, ø oe, æ ae, œ oe), an accent or letter command read as the letter it prints (`spelling_name_text`); callers pass the name unfolded; `respell_emails` moves an e-mail by the name as printed or transliterated, never base-folded (`NameKeys::as_printed`; user ruling 2026-11-05: `joerg.mueller@` is Jörg Müller's, so charter §4 #420's `mueller-group@` example moves to him, `muller-group@` stays); a word's length class is its base spelling's, a literal-vs-transliterated tie stays a tie (`mueller@` beside Hans Mueller and Jörg Müller: the block; user ruling 2026-11-05), and a surname's six-letter prefix never reuses the given name's letters (review rev107a; the last also fixes a pre-existing `andrea.rossi@` on Andrea Andreassen). | — | **108b: the silent lost words counted — the title-page check's third source (brief 108b; OD #953 "108b")** — FIXED (a check; one XML change, item C). What the document's text gives a raw class's or own-directory file's setter before the title page is digested and wanted: `Warning:frontmatter:unclaimed_store` for a word only it wants, an Info `frontmatter:unread_store` for a store no code reads but page furniture, a `\csname` store, an optional argument, or digits from an argument with no counted word. A document end in such an argument is inert in every speculative run (`gullet::speculative_digestion`; 106t's run lost the body before it too). Bounds said in Infos. 5 of the 7 silent dev3 papers now counted (2305.00867, 1011.2770, 2408.05088, 2605.02209, 1401.7920); B, a binding's drop in the document's macro (2503.14354); C, `\@listi`'s size10.clo bodies (2310.07393: 2 Errors → 0, "ampus" → "Campus"). On 2,530 papers: raw-class 14.3 / 1,000 warned, 9.0 false (read against the whole PDF, mostly stores under a switch that is off: vgtc's `\papertype`, `\category`); others 5.7 / 1,000, 3.6 false. CMS papers +3.2 to +3.9 % `instructions:u`, the check's whole-document scan an unheld word starts. ## From `perfect_kernel/KERNEL_CAPABILITIES.md` (2026-11-08)

K23's section, verbatim, and its table row's status cell as it stood; the capability's living doc is the frontmatter plan.

Status cell: OPEN — shadow mode first (user: needs very careful testing); step 1's observer core landed as FM S2a.1 (OD #837, `LATEXML_FM_SHADOW=1`); the resolver, policy table and `ab_audit fmshadow` comparator as FM S2a.2 r1-r7 (shadow only; r4: revtex4_support below the class guard, the S2b pilot candidate; r5: ams_support below the unclassified bar too (no precision sample of its own yet), the comparator's name-note gap closed; S2b gates per family (int63); r6: the main session's decisions 2-3 (a note's links by spelling or as its only marked link; an entry's address group written out), revtex4_support passes the gate on int63 (0.4 % unclassified, 38/39 classified right); r7: the r6 review's findings (live places, a note's links placed once every name is read), a name's mark and separator glyphs read as such, a note's mark answering a `\footnotemark` entry; the other families not yet, SYNC "FM S2a.2")

### K23 — Frontmatter author-block event model

**Model:** none in TeX for "a name" (beyond Perl: Perl emits one creator, KPE #521) — the reason per-shape fixes do not
converge. **Abstraction:** Layer A, a lexer of an author block into events with source spans (Name, Mark, Sep, Affil,
Contact, Note, Layout, Group); Layer B, per binding and syntax only (which commands make which events, the association
policy, the block shape); Layer C, one class-independent resolver recording the rule behind each association; Layer D,
one emission with no relocation pass.

**Landing:** (0) K24's frontmatter-tuple extractor and a snapshot of ~10k frontmatter-bearing papers by family; (1)
Layer A in shadow mode beside today's path, logging disagreements, no output change; (2) one family at a time, new and
small first (OSA/EPL/l-aa, interact, elsarticle/cas, IEEE, acmart, generic article last), byte-identical on the family
corpus plus every frontmatter guard, any intended change a RED→GREEN repro with a ruling; (3) delete the superseded
heuristics in `base_utilities/`.

**Class guard:** shadow disagreement < 0.5% per family before migrating; the 49 frontmatter REDs go green without new
per-shape rules. **Risk:** HIGH; needs very careful testing (user 2026-10-11).

**As landed (FM S2a.1, fms2a1; OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #837).** Layer A's data in shadow mode:
`latexml_engine/src/base_utilities/frontmatter/observer/` — a ledger of the `\author`-family calls (kernel and
`\lx@add@authors*`) with their stamped positions and queue places, the contact/creator API calls after them; a rehearsal
of each block under Layer-B event bindings (the speculative-run engine, nothing kept); the event lexer (Name, Mark,
Sep, Break, Contact, Note, Layout, ApiCall with field spans and ink). Under `LATEXML_FM_SHADOW=1` (unset on the
fleet) the CLI writes `<dest>.fmshadow.json`; without it nothing runs, and with it the XML and the log are
byte-identical: the rehearsal's group reads from a mouth of its own, so an `\aftergroup` of the block's stays inside it
(r2). The model's departures from the design note (a Note carries no mark, a font Layout no span, gap em of the box's
font, `\corref`/`\fnref`/`\thanksref`/`\ead` not bound, projected spans keyed by (block, span), a bare
`\footnotemark` under the kernel `\maketitle`'s `\@fnsymbol` unless a binding declares the title's marks through the
hook `\lx@title@thefootnote` (the kernel's, since 78o r2), a font switch inside a run shown at the next run, an `\input`'s tokens without span)
are listed in OD #837 for S2a.2. r3 (review revfms2a1_2): a container's placement argument is no name (`shown_arguments`),
the kernel's `\lx@newline` a `\centering` or a box rebinds `\\` to is a Break, a stray `&` a column separator (nothing
held: the `&` constructor errs only when built), progress notes silenced in the rehearsal (an `\input` in the field no
longer doubled the log's `(Processing content …` note), a block deferred for want of a journal says so. Not yet: the
resolver and the comparator (S2a.2), any output change (S2b on). Guards `perfect_kernel_batchfms2a1::*`; SYNC_STATUS
"FM S2a.1".

**As landed (FM S2a.2, fms2a2; OD #837 "S2a.2").** Layers B and C in shadow mode: `observer/policy.rs` (the policy
table keyed on (the binding owning `\author`, the hook), each entry citing what it reproduces; since 87a each binding
declares its own conventions at load, read from the state, OD #837 "The conventions' home") and `observer/resolver/`
(rules 1-9 of the design note, each association with its rule), whose tuples the dump carries as `resolved`; the
comparator `ab_audit fmshadow ROOT` (per-family table with the gate number, a row per classified/unclassified
disagreement, `--re-resolve` to resolve a dump's events again offline). The class guard is not met: the census numbers
and the PDF checks are in SYNC_STATUS "FM S2a.2". Guards `perfect_kernel_batchfms2a2::*`. r2 (review revfms2a2, three
quarters of r1's unclassified share the resolver's own defects): the resolver fixed by defect class (whole note texts,
whole fuzzy labels, `author:N`, Perl's prefix fallback, legends by their leading Mark, names lines by evidence, marks
mid-line and trailing, the API intake, contact roles, authblk's `\AB@thenote`, today's joined lines and contact grid,
a replacing `\author`), the policy key's class fallback (report/book title-page marks), and the comparator made honest
(the block compared, joined items only, classified = an adopted ruling's change or a PDF-confirmed XML-wrong class
listed by name; elimination counted as today's, not a change). r3 (r2's classified sample 69 % right; the revtex4_support
and ams_support unclassified papers read against their PDFs): marked entries of one call parted by their marks, glued
labels parted by the marks shown, fuzzy labels by surname and with their macros written out, e-mails by evidence (a
note's by spelling, author-order lists for every class), the ORCID links, `\cite` marks and environment arguments of the
lexer, a sole author's orphans, title notes, and six more PDF-confirmed XML-wrong classes (OD #837 "S2a.2 r3").
r4 (the r3 revtex4_support and ams_support residual against the PDFs; review revfms2a2_3): `observer/resolver/` and
`ab_audit/fmshadow/` split by stage, every file below 1,000 lines (byte-identical shadow dumps); today's attachment
reproduced where the shadow had invented one (an empty entry's role and its place in today's contact pile, a
`\lx@affiliation@withsup` line's first superscript, today's contact grid before the labels, annotations before any
author), a prefix-marked name's notes, ORCID links printing a name, the lexer's `\thanks` inside a superscript and
numeric box arguments, the comparator's `unobserved` bucket and one more PDF-confirmed XML-wrong class (welded
legend lines), the review's ledger nits (OD #837 "S2a.2 r4"). revtex4_support is the first family under the class
guard; the precision sample and the census are in SYNC_STATUS "FM S2a.2". r5 (r4's classified sample, review

#### Row XP — A detail-less XPath failure in post is a resource failure, not 100+ Errors

- **A detail-less XPath failure in post is a resource failure, not 100+ Errors** (runs 362/363): on the fleet, post's `ltx:XMath` lookups failed "(no libxml2 error detail)" 87-501 times per paper (latexml_post/src/document.rs:959, :1031) until `TooManyErrors` — 8 of run 363's 35 E→F (1008.4986, 1712.03782, 1910.13564, 2101.04438, 2208.07188, 2305.17269, 2401.10096, 2609.06115);
- each converts locally with 1-14 Errors and no such failure.
- A trivial relative path cannot fail syntactically, so a NULL result without a libxml2 error is libxml2's own allocation failure inside a long-lived child (C `malloc`, outside the Rust allocator hook that turns an allocation failure into `Fatal:oom`).
- Fix: route it to the same cause-carrying `Fatal:oom` path (one Fatal, the child recycled, retried per the 2026-10-15 reused-child ruling) instead of an Error per node;
- keep the Error for failures that carry a libxml2 message.
- Mechanism CONFIRMED (68m step 1, 2026-10-16; evidence ~/data/pk_agents/main/scratch_356a0c47/68m_step1/RESULTS.md): exhausting glibc malloc under RLIMIT_AS makes rust-libxml =0.3.22 `node_evaluate_checked` return exactly "XPath evaluation failed (no libxml2 error detail)", code 2 (XML_ERR_NO_MEMORY), domain 12, message None, on libxml2 2.15 and 2.9.14, where a bad XPath (`ltx:XMath[`) gives "Invalid expression" (code 1207);
- a fresh-process `ulimit -v` sweep of 2101.04438 (1.2-7.0 GB) never reproduces it (the Rust side fails first), so it needs a reused child.
- FIXED (80m): latexml_post document.rs `end_on_libxml_oom` — a detail-less `XML_ERR_NO_MEMORY` (`is_libxml_oom`) ends through the binary's allocation-failure hook (`std::alloc::handle_alloc_error`: one `Fatal:oom:alloc_failed`, exit 137, the worker's `exit_report` cause, the child recycled and the paper retried), after an allocation-free context line;
- a failure with a message stays an Error.
- Guard `latexml_post document::tests::libxml_oom_is_told_from_an_xpath_error` (the fleet condition itself needs a reused child under RLIMIT_AS and is not reproduced in a test)
- State: **FIXED 80m**
- S
- runs 362/363 bullet below; memory feedback_rulings_2026_10_15

#### 21 — biblatex `\printbibliography` filters

- biblatex `\printbibliography` filters: every list printed every entry
- State: PARTLY FIXED 77a (OD #762, KPE #983): `type`/`subtype`/`keyword`/`category` and their `not*` forms narrow the `.bbl` list in the order given, ANDed, a repeated key again (`type`/`subtype` twice is biblatex's error); category membership is decided once the document is digested, so a late `\addtocategory` counts, and a category list's entries are deferred there and built only if kept (77a r4: no entry digested that no list keeps, none built again in the hidden list, which holds only unprinted entries; an entry two lists keep is typeset twice, as in pdflatex); `\bibbycategory` per category under its `\defbibheading` title; each list is its own refsection's, and a refsection's citations link to its own list (`inlist`/`lists` `refsection<n>`; 77a r4, witnesses 2308.00515, 2507.16315); "Empty bibliography" as biblatex; an entry has one label number in every list of its refsection, read from the list's own copy (its datalist place; print order with `defernumbers=true`, `resetnumbers`), a `shorthand` entry its shorthand and no number, on the `.bib` route too (77a r6; biblatex only: under natbib a `.bib` `shorthand`/`labelalpha` field is raw data and no label, 77a r5); biblatex's `\bibliographystyle` is ignored with biblatex's error as a Warning (blx-compat.def:13-17), so a leftover call after `\printbibliography` no longer turns the list into a `.bst`'s and drops its alphabetic labels (77a r6); the entries no list printed are built hidden, so their citations and `\fullcite` resolve (2405.19762); arXiv 2406.00136 21 + 21 → 4 + 17 with pdflatex's labels, 2406.11014. Witnesses: TL `doc/latex/biblatex/examples/13-19,94`, 2406.00136. Reach: 1 of a 6,000-paper arXiv sample; 18 of the 38,734 papers of 2405+2406 use a filter or `\addtocategory`, all with a `.bbl`. **Open (unmodelled)**: a category filled by code the binding never runs (hooks, bibmacros, drivers, formats, cite-command code, a parameter name) is not filtered — the list prints unfiltered, an Info says so; `check=`/`\defbibcheck`, `filter=`/`\defbibfilter`, `segment=`, `sorting=` per list, `omitnumbers`, `prefixnumbers`/`labelprefix`, biblatex's "Split bibliography detected" Warning, and filters on the no-`.bbl` (`.bib`) route; the `.bib` route numbers a `style=alphabetic` list where biber prints alphabetic labels, and ignores the package-level `sorting=` (`sorting=none` lists alphabetically; 77a r6 review probe br/b_none) (both predate 77a). **Open (a)**: refsections opened at headings (`refsection=chapter|section`) are not counted — each `\printbibliography` prints every refsection's list and their citations misroute (arXiv 2406.01695: 8 `\printbibliography` → 96 lists, 4,888 items, 566/566 citations outside their chapter's list; minimal trigger = 77a r4 review probe q3: `report`, `\usepackage[refsection=chapter]{biblatex}`, two `\chapter`s each citing and calling `\printbibliography[heading=subbibliography]` with biber's two-refsection `.bbl` — pdflatex prints each chapter's own list, we print both lists twice); model biblatex's `\blx@refpatch@sect` heading patch (biblatex.sty `\blx@patchsection@switch`, a no-op in the binding) so each heading bumps the refsection and its `CITE_UNIT`. **Open (b)**: the hidden list digests whole entries, so the errors their data raises are raised although pdflatex never typesets them (pinned: `biblatex_filtered_out_entries_errors`, 2 errors, pdflatex 0; review probe e3). Faithful route (77a r4 review): build hidden-list entries label-only (labelnumber, labelalpha/extraalpha/shorthand, labelname, labeldate/extradate, labeltitle) and digest whole entries only for keys a full-entry citation typesets (`\fullcite`, `\footfullcite`, the `\citetitle`/`\citeurl` fields)

#### caption separator vs a copy's own label

- caption separator vs a copy's own label: the separator is the binding's model of the class's `\@makecaption` (`\format@title@<type>`'s close), so a routed copy's (or a raw class's) `\fnum@<type>` that carries its class's punctuation meets the binding's (or the kernel's ": "): "Fig. 1.—.— " (1106.2565 emulateapj-rtx4.cls:911 + the emulateapj binding's ".— ", astro-ph0204283), "Figure 1..— " (1505.06804), "Table 1:: " (astro-ph0601469, astro-ph0603093), "Figure 1.: " (astro-ph0101121), "Fig. 1.—: " (1402.7103, PDF "Fig. 1.— " from proto.cls:1226 `#1\ #2`) — 7 of the 78h A/B's 322 papers, worse than int47a's binding label
- State: RULED 2026-10-21: the separator is the binding's (default ": " as Perl), and a copy's caption format (`\fnum@<type>`) is the binding's too — only its declared text names (`\figurename`) reach the label (OD #731 rule 14); no raw `\@makecaption` is read. The 7 doubled separators of 78h r1 are gone (78h r2) — closed

#### K11's line rule (OD #662) after 79a r2 — a store printed in one line with another value of the class's, or glued to its text, stays the class's

- K11's line rule (OD #662) after 79a r2 — a store printed in one line with another value of the class's, or glued to its text, stays the class's: (a) a store kept the class's when the class is loaded (another value in its line printing text then) is not judged again at `\maketitle`, so a document that empties that value still leaves the store in the class's line;
- (b) a line whose other value the document leaves empty hands the store on and leaves the class's text around it in the body — zzfmf.cls's `{\itshape \@issueinfo; DOI \@doi\par}` without `\issueinfo` leaves ";
- DOI" (the control of `perfect_kernel_batch79a::a_store_in_one_line_with_a_document_value_stays_the_class_line`; int68a the same): the label before a store is taken out with it only for an abstract, keywords or a classification (`labelled_api`);
- (c) a store printed outside any group is never in a class's line (the group bound kept from 68x);
- (d) `glued_to_class_text` exempts only `\href`'s first argument as a link target, so WileyASNA-v1.cls's `\burlalt{http://dx.doi.org/\@doi}{…}` keeps `\@doi` the class's (1 census paper).
- Review rev79a findings 2, 4, Q1
- State: open (accepted unless a paper shows (a) or (d))

#### A raster graphic's explicit size comes out at 72/72.27 of it, rounded up to a whole pixel

- A raster graphic's explicit size comes out at 72/72.27 of it, rounded up to a whole pixel: `image_graphicx_sizer`'s pixel branch (`latexml_core/src/util/image.rs`, `apply_graphicx_ops` with `units_per_bp = DPI/72.27`) multiplies graphicx's bp values (`parse_graphicx_options`, `to_bp`) by pixels per pt, as Perl does (Util/Image.pm:162-163 `to_bp`, :237 `ceil($a1 * $dppt)` with `$dppt = DPI/72.27`; a Perl-origin defect, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #1142): `\includegraphics[width=114.6pt,height=1cm]{x.png}` is 114.187pt (158px) where TeX's box is 114.6pt, so two such panels stay one row of REVTeX `[preprint,twocolumn]`'s 229pt column where pdflatex breaks them (review rev78x3 finding 6; probes `rvpt.tex`, `rvpt2.tex`).
- The vector branch is TeX's (`graphicx_box_pt`, 78z).
- Direction: an explicit `width=`/`height=` is the box's size in pt, as TeX's;
- reach every raster with a stated size (box and row widths shift by ≤ 0.4 %)
- State: open

#### Panel grid markup

- **Panel grid markup** (grid design payload 3): the XML says a float's rows only implicitly (the runs between `ltx:break`s);
- missing are a panel's stated width (Perl subcaption.sty.ltxml:60-66 stores `width` and never emits it), spans outside a `tabular`, the column a panel takes across rows, gallery or matrix (int61a sets 1 of 10 labelled matrices as the PDF).
- Options (study Q4): (a) today's elements plus attributes — `panelwidth`/`panelspan` on a panel, `panelrows`/`panelcols` and `ltx_panels_gallery`/`ltx_panels_matrix` on the float (LaTeXML.model `figure_attributes`; the XSLT maps them to CSS custom properties as `@backgroundcolor`);
- (b) a row wrapper element;
- (c) a table grid.
- Sizing S1/S2/S3, a two-column class's `\columnwidth`, matrix detection by heuristic or `tabular` only (design study task #360);
- CSS in LaTeXML.css and ar5iv-css at the goal's end
- State: RULED 2026-10-22: today's elements plus attributes (`panelwidth`/`panelspan` on panels, `panelrows`/`panelcols` + `ltx_panels_gallery`/`ltx_panels_matrix` on the float, XSLT → CSS custom properties); sizing keeps the panels' relative sizes within a figure (no per-image cap that skews them — precomputed sizing values as CSS custom properties; design proposal owed) — after the grid rule payload. MARKUP FIXED (79c, OD #887): `annotate_panel_grid` (after `arrange_panels` and `collapse_float`) states `panelrows`/`panelcols`/the class on floats of 2+ panels, `panelwidth` where the width is the real geometry's and stated (`panel_width_is_stated`; never a guess), a matrix only on a `tabular` of panels or 2+ rows agreeing panel for panel (widths within 0.01 of the line: 0.0-0.01 score alike on the oracle, 0.02 adds a false matrix) with spanning rows, and sub-captions on none of the panels or on exactly one whole row (79c r2); nested groups their own; schema `Panel.attributes` in `Common.attributes` (panels are any element) and `PanelGrid.attributes` on figure/table/float; XSLT `--ltx-panel-rows/cols` on the float's element, `--ltx-panel-width/span` on its `div.ltx_flex_cell`. Oracle (453): study matrix 42 → ours 20 matrix / 22 gallery (16 text-labelled matrices), study gallery 411 → 405 gallery / 5 matrix (grids captioned on one row only) / 1 none; rows 429, shapes 422 unchanged. A/B int71a → 79c (1,907): E 6,680 = 6,680, F 20 = 20, W 59,351 = 59,351, words equal; with the markup stripped 1,855 papers byte-identical, the 52 others the label item's (row above). Sizing RULED 2026-10-23: the float states `panelline` (its line ÷ the PDF's running-text column) and `panelfill` (widest row's Σ `panelwidth`); a panel renders at `panelwidth × panelline` of the theme column ÷ max(1, `panelfill × panelline`); no CSS rule reads them yet (goal-end CSS). 79c r2 (review rev79c): a `tabular` cell counts only for a graphic 24pt+ wide and tall (13 data tables lose the matrix), sub-captions allow a matrix only on exactly one whole row (6 partial-caption grids → galleries), a beside-content collapse drops the inner's widths, a `width=`-only graphic panel states its stated width, the grid attributes typed numeric and the XSLT emits numbers only; guards `perfect_kernel_batch79c::*`, `integration::panel_grid_markup_states_only_numbers`. Residuals: text-labelled matrices (label rows are `ltx:p` panels), a `\resizebox` of measured content states no width (its stated width is not on the element), a raster inside a sub-float body keeps KPE #1142's rounding in its sub-float's width; a float held in a minipage — FIXED 79f (the page outside the box, 0.5 as the PDF); a `\captionof` figure gets no grid markup (not a float environment; Perl alike); class pages declared — FIXED 79f (OD #887). #### An author's mark whose label holds any control sequence prints nothing

- An author's mark whose label holds any control sequence prints nothing: the mark reader reads the label from the operand's unexpanded text (`author_macros.rs:198` `clean_frontmatter_labels(&operand.to_string(),…)`), which no list answers, so `\textsuperscript{\relax 3}` loses its "3" (pdflatex "Ann Alpha3"), `\textsuperscript{\one}` and `\textsuperscript{\protect 2}` their "1" and "2", `\textsuperscript{\relax 1\thanks{a}}` and `\textsuperscript{\protect 2\thanks{b}}` keep the note but lose the label, as do `1\protect\protect\thanks{b}`, `{\protect}1\thanks{f}` and `\protect\protect\thanks{c}`, and `1,\protect\one\thanks{n}` gives `<sup>1</sup>` where pdflatex prints "1,1".
- No error is reported.
- int62a, int63a and 78v alike (older than 78v);
- synthetic, no census paper.
- Fix: read the label from what it typesets (expand macros, drop an unexpandable `\relax`/`\protect`) before matching it.
- Review rev78v2 finding 6a, widened by rev78v3 finding 3 (probes `rev78v3/q/pl.tex`, `pra.tex`, `prc.tex`, `rev78v2/p/sh3.tex`);
- RED repro `sectioning-frontmatter/author_mark_relax_before_its_label`
- State: open

#### Author marks

- Author marks: inside a note macro's body, a `\protect` before another note-making macro (`\newcommand\fnL{\footnotemark}`, `\newcommand\fnB{7\protect\fnL}`, `Lu L\textsuperscript{\fnB}`) is not label-safe, so the macro is taken out whole and its "7" lands in the name text: `L7[mark]` where 78v r2 gave `L[mark]<sup>7</sup>` (pdflatex "L7§").
- `prints_beside_a_note` (`frontmatter/classify/preprocess.rs:199-202`) lets a `\protect` through only before a note command, where `split_mark_notes` (`:71-74`) also takes one before a note-making macro.
- Markup only, nothing printed lost;
- 78v r3 = int63a;
- synthetic, no census paper.
- Fix: accept a `\protect` before a `makes_a_note` macro in the body loop as `split_mark_notes` does, a row in `perfect_kernel_batch78v::author_protect_thanks_and_note_macro_inside_a_mark`, OD #881 "before a note command or note macro".
- Review rev78v3 finding 1 (probe `rev78v3/q/prb.tex`)
- State: open

#### Complete breakage at 100 % (user 2026-10-24)

- **Complete breakage at 100 % (user 2026-10-24)**: a missing image, a `\ref` or `\cite` with no target, where pdflatex resolves it.

- (c) a macro store a `\ps@` style prints under another name (`\def\correspondingauthor#1{\gdef\@corr{#1}}` + `\def\ps@firststyle{\def\@oddfoot{\@corr}}`) is no store (its readers are no title-page code);

- (e) a register printed under a switch the class leaves false (`\ifshowca … \the\correspondingauthor\fi` + `\showcafalse`) still makes the note — `printed_register` knows only the `\if\relax\the\X\relax` test (RED `toks_store_printed_under_a_false_switch_is_no_note`, rev95d S4; fix: read the printing branch live or dead, as `reads_of_branches`);
- (f) a preamble `\fancypagestyle{firststyle}{\fancyfoot[L]{Preprint}}` that drops the register still makes the note — the style is read at the class's end (RED `toks_store_page_style_the_document_redefines_is_no_note`, rev95d S5; fix: re-read `\ps@<style>` at `harvest_stores`);
- (g) a setter's own store no macro reads, or one read by a page style the class never selects, is still rerouted — kept for astlb.cls:361-372's `\@shorttitle`, printed only by a running head its top level sets (1202.2889's `<toctitle>`) —, so a dead store (`\def\keywords#1{\gdef\@keywords{#1}}`, nothing reading `\@keywords`) is an invented item;
- whether an uncalled macro runs is not read off the code (`read_where_nothing_runs` takes any reader the never-running code reaches: cesj.sty's unselected `\cejpmaketitle`, physics0510010);
- (h) a label printed through a macro that reads the store (ujp.sty:107/:421 `\udkpacs~\udkpacsnum`, `\def\udkpacsnum{\@pacs}`; 1906.11531), or in a group that is a command's argument with layout the label reader does not take (CSML.cls:227-229's `\@footnotetext{\textit{\ACMCCSname:}\enspace\@ACMCCS.}`, the stand-in tpccs.cls:12; 1608.04592, 1602.00476), is not read: the classification is unnamed where the class prints "PACS", "2012 ACM CCS:" (review rev95d-r2 finding 3; charter-correct, better hidden than mislabelled);
- (i) `\fancycenter{a}{b}{c}` called in a style body outside a field (pdflatex typesets it where `\pagestyle` runs the style) prints nothing — the style runs nothing (int124a and Perl the same);
- (j) an unlabelled date among labelled ones is handed on as it is set, the labelled ones at `\maketitle` (`labelled_api` defers only a named one), so it comes first: medima's shape with `\@finalform` printed bare gives the final-form date before "Received";
- (k) a raw class's own store only an uncalled macro reads (`\def\makecover{… \@keywords …}`, never called) is still an invented item — the raw package's test (`read_where_nothing_runs`) is not applied to classes, where it lost bfhthesis's `\@department` (read through a `\@titlefooterleft` a scrlayer layer prints) and dmtcs's keywords (0905.1102) (RED `raw_class_store_only_an_uncalled_macro_reads_is_no_item`, rev95d p9);
- (l) a role's default kept because its word is printed right before the store (`ClassReaders::prints_default_name_before`: wiley2sp.cls:296-302's `\receivedname\ignorespaces\@received`, 1211.4450, 1306.2559; zaa.cls:513-515's `\AtEndDocument` hook `Received \rm\@received`, 1902.07328, the date also printed there, the class path's double print of (k)) leaves that printed label in the title-page deposit ("Received:" over nothing, as before 95d-r2; `perfect_kernel_batch95d::a_date_with_no_label_read_keeps_its_default_only_where_printed`), and a date stored right after a `;` or `,` (no label of its own: `\@revised; \@accepted`, iaus.cls's `No. \@volume, \@pubyear`, 0707.3527) leaves that separator there, the unnamed date first by (j);

- **Per-entry wraps of nested containers, and an `\href` around entries (94b r7 review residuals; synthetic, triage 2026-10-31: RED catalog, no fix round)**: (1) a container inside a kernel text font command with more entries after its group loses the container's font (`\textbf{\emph{$^1$…\\ $^2$…}} $^3$…`: bold kept, italic lost; `emitter/plan.rs` `entry_forms`/`container_wrap`);
- (2) `events/fonts.rs` `font_declaration` matches by name, so a redefined `\bfseries` (`\quad`) is copied into every entry (the B2 class: what prints once copied into each entry) — compare meanings (the S3 `x_equals` pattern);

- int111d, 2,578 scored papers: 84.3 % clean;
- name defects 4.3 % (several names in one creator 40 names, e-mail/ORCID in a name 29 papers, affiliation as a name 18, missing author 18, IEEE membership in a name 5, `\author` but no creator 4);
- a hand-typeset title page with no `\author` and no creators 6.9 % (178; all 8 sampled print their authors; Perl the same — a beyond-Perl capability, needs a ruling);
- block-level contacts only 4.5 % (may be FIDELITY_CHARTER §4's \"else the block\", e.g. llncs's one shared `\institute`).

- per-class flag samples all real.

- **Before the flip: the switch-on leftovers (one batch after the safety net's; all `LATEXML_FM_EMIT=2` only, the pre-flip default output unaffected; `=2` the default since item (38))** — OPEN.
- **DEL1: `=0` is gone from the tests and the switch is fixed at `=2`** (`fm_emit_mode` a constant, `LATEXML_FM_EMIT` unread; OD #980): the `red_at_two` guards asserting only `=0` deleted with their orphaned fixtures, the guards that assert something at `=2` first keep it and lose their `=0` tail, the explicit `=0`/mode-1 halves removed, `expect(off, two)` → `two` (the RED rows kept in `FRONTMATTER_REDS_AT_EMIT2.tsv`, marked).
- **DEL2: the switch and the old driver are deleted** (OD #980): `fm_emit_mode`/`set_fm_emit_override` gone, every `=0` arm collapsed (emitter, resolver `emitting`/`whole_entries`, rehearsal, shadow `reads_now`, ledger overflow, `frontmatter_stores`), `add_authors_calls_sectioned`/`split_author_calls`/`line_kinds.rs` and every `author_block/`/`fallback.rs` item only they reached removed by the compiler (the IJCAI split kept as `author_macros.rs` `ijcai_split_call`);

- a superscript after an emitted entry's label is its text (1812.07638's `(ECT$^*$)`);
- an address decides only its own ink (2203.10777, 1210.0352, 1605.03019);

#### 98d: `\end{X}` over a `\begingroup` opened inside X (kernel; SYNC "The unclaimed-text net" R0; OD #965, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #1207)

- **98d: `\end{X}` over a `\begingroup` opened inside X (kernel; SYNC "The unclaimed-text net" R0; OD #965, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #1207)** — FIXED: as TeX scopes it (tex.web §1063-1069, latex.ltx:15384-15393), a mode-bearing DefEnvironment's `\end{X}` met while plain `\begingroup` groups stand above X's own frame pops the innermost one, ends X (its paragraph, mode, boxing level, own `beforeAfterGroup` work) and keeps X's frame as a plain group with X's other local values for the next `\endgroup` (`stomach.rs` `end_environment_over_plain_groups`, frame surgery `state.rs` `unbind_value_in_frame`/`replace_value_in_frame`; WISDOM #102);
- another environment's `\end{Y}`, a frameless `\end{X}`, an `\end{X}` over a raw `\newenvironment`'s `\begin{Y}` (the open environment is Y; repro `end_over_a_raw_environments_begin_stays_an_error`), a box frame between, a math environment or a box-group begin keep the Error.
- Perl errs on the shape (Stomach.pm:544-550), but on the raa witnesses loads no raw class (OmniBus) and keeps the abstract.
- A/B vs int132a1 over 1,140 papers (`pop98d.tsv`: the 12 witnesses, the 104 dev20k/longtail dropped-deposit papers, the 26 dev20k papers with an `\end{` Error, 998 controls from `popint125a_s10k`): 41 changed, 0 worse, E 4,215 → 4,213, F 13 → 13, words +8,070;

- the 54 eptcs dropped-deposit papers unchanged;
- dropped deposits 119 → 80;
- `fm_checks.py` 1-7 clean;
- creators identical except astro-ph0508574 (cjaa; markup only: its two institutes now in an attribute-less `<ltx:text>`, the `\\` before cjaa's `\email` a trailing `<break/>`).
- Lost-words over the 38 raa-lineage witnesses 38 → 3.
- Round 2 (review rev98d S1): the branch also refuses when the open environment is not X or a group above X records another (LaTeX's `\@badend`);
- re-A/B, both sides on `vendor_dumpsint132a`: 40 changed vs int132a1 (E 4,215 → 4,214), vs round 1 only 2101.04970, now byte-identical to int132a1 (ntheorem's `\theoremprework{\begin{changemargin}…}` raw group above `\end{fabsmfabstract}`).

#### Token-stack RAII (int131fix follow-up)

- **Token-stack RAII (int131fix follow-up)** — `invoke_token` (stomach.rs) pushes `token_stack` and pops it on its normal exits only;
- the recovery arms now clear the stack (`stomach::clear_token_stack`), but an error caught below them (a speculative digestion that discards its `Err`, a binding's `catch`) still leaves its entries, and a later digestion can trip `MAXSTACK` early with a ghost `Stomach:Recursion`.
- Fix: an RAII pop guard beside `local_current_token_guard`, every exit of `invoke_token` popping once.
- Witnesses 2410.07647/2105.02599 (fixed by the clear);
- 2502.10606 (a speculative title-page run's discarded `Stomach:Recursion`, 106s at int135b) fixed by `stomach::StomachMark`, which the speculative runs now take and put back (row 106s) — a binding's own `catch` is still uncovered.
- Pre-existing, from review rev135c (synthetic): an `Err` inside `invoke_conditional` (a recursion while reading a test) leaks the localized `if_frames` entry (local_assignments.rs:68), so `open_conditional_count() == 0` (stomach.rs fragment yields under memory pressure) never holds again — `StomachMark` could truncate it too;
- speculative runs advance `if_count` toward `iflimit`;
- a false test opened in one rehearsed block and closed in the next takes the wrong branch (base the same).
- State: OPEN

#### 95b r3 review should-fixes (rev95b-r3 SHIP, 2026-10-31; none a regression or a loss)

- **95b r3 review should-fixes (rev95b-r3 SHIP, 2026-10-31; none a regression or a loss)** — (S1, moot since DEL5 deleted the setter watch) a setter's input mark can stay open after its expansion ends: `state.rs` `input_unread_since` (~:2436-2449) compares the mouth's depth and read position but not which mouth, and token-list digestion runs on `Mouth::default()` (position (0,0,0,0), mouth.rs:164-175), so a mark taken at the end of a digested list reads open in a later default-mouth context;

#### AMS contact setters under raw amsart derivatives (next batch; 86c r1, reverted)

- **AMS contact setters under raw amsart derivatives (next batch; 86c r1, reverted)**: a raw class read over the AMS binding re-dresses `\address`, `\curraddr`, `\email`, `\urladdr` as stores only its own `\@maketitle` prints (stacs.cls:294-298, stacs_proc.cls, rmmcart.cls; m2an.cls, edpsmath.cls, pcms-l.cls:481 `\csname @address\number\addresscount@`), and our deposit of that `\@maketitle` is dropped, so the addresses are printed nowhere (0902.1737, 0802.2833).
- A lock on the four against the files (`"{cs}:locked@files"`) keeps them, but alone it imposes the binding's argument meaning on classes whose arguments differ — reverted until it ships with: (1) a stacs/stacs_proc binding: `\address[ref]{…}` an address with no name (the key is matched against `\author[ref]`, never printed; the lock made it a visible `<contact name="uniroma">`, 0902.1737's `lab1`–`lab4` in 0802.2833), and `\author[ref]{short}{long}` (stacs.cls:256) the long name as the person (today a stray long-name paragraph);
- (2) a pcms-l `\auth` binding (pcms-l.cls:203, reached through the paper's `\renewcommand{\auth}{\secdef\@auth\@sauth}`, pcmslmod-modified.tex:89), else the address goes to a nameless creator ahead of all (math0505518, math0602226);
- (3) OD #803's empty-shell drop extended to the deposit's footnotes and pubnotes whose content became contacts (m2an_mod.cls:118 `\footnote{\box\@b@ddrss@}`: an empty `<note role="footnote" mark="2">` in 2502.17950, 2309.08130, 2411.09498, math0701227; an empty `<pubnote/>` in 2305.18054, 2604.25825);
- (4) the lock with a wrapper opt-in that lets a file's redefinition through when it still calls the binding through a `\let`-saved copy (epigamath.sty:227-232, 2206.02674, 2304.03539, 2305.12241; MyCommA.sty:530-533, 2602.16384), where S1: the call must be real — at brace depth 0 and outside a conditional, not a mention (`sectioning-frontmatter/{ams_raw_class_address_setter_conditional, ams_raw_class_address_setter_deferred_call}`, review probes `p_cond`, `p_wrapstore`);
- S2: a wrapper installed with `\let\address\wr@address` takes the same test on `let_i`'s `:locked@files` path (`ams_package_let_wrapper_keeps_both`, `p_letwrap`);
- S3: a class's `\AtBeginDocument{\renewcommand\address…}` runs at the document's origin (OD #596) and bypasses the lock — fix or record (`ams_raw_class_address_atbegindocument`, `p_atbegin`).
- Boundaries the lock must keep: the document's own redefinition (1212.1375, 2206.01924; `ams_document_address_redefinition_stays`), a package's wrapper (`ams_package_wrapping_setters_keeps_both`).
- Repros (no guard): `sectioning-frontmatter/ams_raw_class_address_setters_stored` (RED) and the six above;
- the r1 code is branch `pk/86c-r1-backup` (state.rs `calls_locked_meaning`, ams_support_sty.rs).
- Its census (`ab_66bundle/pop86c.tsv`, 880 AMS-derived/redefining papers + a 292-paper amsart sample): 14 changed, also 1909.04033, 2001.07042, 2609.01974 and 2411.00367 (d-omega3.sty: E 18 → 9, `\organization`/`\orcid` still lost).
- Review `agent_reports/2026-10-28_review_86c_r1.md` (B1–B3, S1–S3);

- its guard must assert `@name` (the `creators()` helper cannot see it);

#### float.sty's `\restylefloat{<type>}` of a kernel float (figure, table) records the style only (float_sty.rs `record_float_style`), so without caption the caption keeps …

- float.sty's `\restylefloat{<type>}` of a kernel float (figure, table) records the style only (float_sty.rs `record_float_style`), so without caption the caption keeps the format it had: a ruled figure "Fig. 1: text" under IEEEtran (kernel ": " under article) where float.sty's `\floatc@ruled` prints "Fig. 1 text" (review rev78y probe3/rs.tex);
- the plain style's ": " happens to match.
- float.sty's own `\newfloat` types get the style's format (float_sty.rs `\lx@float@newfloat`, Perl float.sty.ltxml:50-53, whose plain format puts a space before the text and whose `boxed` style reads " " where float.sty prints ": ").
- Fix: give an unstarred `\restylefloat` the `\newfloat` type's style format.
- Also: a `\restylefloat` in the document body is not seen, since the class format is installed at `\begin{document}` keeping only the types restyled by then (caption_sty.rs `class_makecaption`, `\lx@class@floatsty@kept`): under IEEEtran `\restylefloat{figure}` before a second figure leaves "Fig. 2.
- " where pdflatex prints "Fig. 2: ", and `\restylefloat*{table}` after a `\restylefloat{table}` leaves "TABLE II: " where pdflatex prints "TABLE II" over the text (78z probe `scratch_78z/rs/rs.tex`; the preamble forms are right, guard `perfect_kernel_batch78y::ieeetran_restylefloat_table_keeps_float_sty_format`).
- 78y r2
- State: OPEN (pre-existing; found at 78y r2)

#### A preload's options do not reach a package another preload already loaded, in either order, so until 78z the class caption fonts knob could not be combined with ar5iv.sty

- A preload's options do not reach a package another preload already loaded, in either order, so until 78z the class caption fonts knob could not be combined with ar5iv.sty: `--preload=[classcaptionfonts]latexml.sty --preload=ar5iv.sty` sets the knob but loses every option ar5iv.sty passes to latexml.sty (`ids`, `rawstyles`, `rawclasses`, `magnify`/`zoomout`, the limits — `\documentclass{paper}` falls to OmniBus with `Warning:missing_file` where ar5iv alone reads paper.cls raw; the magnify PIs and `<p>`/autoref ids gone), and `--preload=ar5iv.sty --preload=[classcaptionfonts]latexml.sty` sets no knob (no `\ds@classcaptionfonts` runs).
- Mechanism: `core_interface.rs:1085-1092` puts a preload's options into `opt@<name>.<ext>` only, `InputDefinitionOptions.options` stays empty, so binding/content.rs `apply_repeat_options` returns at its first test (`content.rs:602`);
- the repeat load is not re-read.
- Perl alike in effect (Core.pm:333-335 passes `options =>`; a repeat load only reports the "Option clash" Info, Package.pm:2586-2590, and is not re-read, Package.pm:2357).
- Relaxing every declared `\ds@` handler after `\ProcessOptions` is Perl's (Package.pm:2479-2481, ported at `content.rs:3339-3342`; latex.ltx:18566-18568 empties only the used ones) and not the cause.
- Fix for the knob (78z): ar5iv.sty declares `classcaptionfonts`/`noclasscaptionfonts` and passes the one given to latexml.sty with its own options (`ar5iv_sty.rs` `pass_options`), so `--preload=[classcaptionfonts]ar5iv.sty` sets the knob and keeps ar5iv's options (OD #884).
- Pre-existing;
- reachable since 78y r2 (review rev78y2 finding 1, probes `k1_*`, `k2_*`)
- State: the class caption fonts knob FIXED 78z (guards `perfect_kernel_batch78z::{ar5iv_classcaptionfonts_option_sets_the_knob, ar5iv_classcaptionfonts_option_keeps_ar5iv_options}`); the general repeat-preload behaviour OPEN

#### Determinism (det354 B2)

- Determinism (det354 B2): the `xmlns:*` declarations on the root `<document>` are in the order of the model's namespace table (`common/model.rs:1244-1254`, a `SymHashMap` at `:55`, → `document.rs:774-830`), not the order the document first uses them, as Perl declares them (Document.pm:1393-1394,1874): `\usepackage{fontawesome,tikz}` then `\faTimes … \tikz\draw (0,0)--(1,1);` declares `xmlns:svg` before `xmlns:aria`, though `aria` is used first (trigger det354 `repro/ns.tex`; Perl declares `svg` only).
- Stable today across int32a–int64a, since the symbols are created at init — a latent binary-to-binary instability, and golden churn on every SVG+aria document when changed.
- Fix: a document-order walk, with the prefixes of streaming-spilled content sorted after it;
- or sort by prefix (simpler, not Perl's).
- Not in 78w (main-session decision 2026-10-22)
- State: open

### From "Rust Error Fixes" (entry bodies)

- **An `\xymatrix` cell opening with `\xy` lost the wrapper's conditional (82f)**: xy_sty.rs's `\xy` wrapper (`\if\inxy@\lx@xy@svgnested\else\lx@xy@svg\fi\lx@xy@original`, LaTeXML's own) at a cell head was expanded by the alignment's peek and its conditional was lost before the `\else` in xymatrix's two-pass `\halign` — 10 errors for `$\xymatrix{\xy *{a} \endxy}$`, conditional-limit Fatals in 1109.4916 and 1611.07444 (a raw `\halign` keeps such a conditional: probed). The wrapper opens with `\relax`, as `\endxy`'s does. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::an_xymatrix_cell_may_open_with_xy`.
- **A display's `$$` deferred past an author's open group (82g)**: tex.web §1193 + §1064-1065 `off_save` closes a group opened in the formula at its math shift; done for inline formulas (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #553), a display's end waited for the group's own end, which a typo (`$$ H^k{X,\C/\Z) $$`, 1309.7562) never reaches — 101 errors and a Fatal, pdflatex 1. A `$$` display outside alignments carries `DISPLAY_FORMULA` and takes the same path (`formula_reachable`). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::a_display_math_shift_closes_a_group_opened_in_it`.
- **`\newcommand{\cs[n]}{…}` (82h)**: KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #1192; `LaTeXDefToken` adds latex.ltx's `\@testopt{…}0` step (1312.5793: 90 errors and a Fatal → 0).
- **`\pdftexbanner` empty (82i)**: Perl's is too (pdfTeX.pool.ltxml:93); it now names the runtime tree's TeX Live year and that release's versions, which 2309.17381 parses for the year (an error-limit Fatal before). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::pdftexbanner_names_the_texlive_year`.
- **A package by its installed path missed its binding (82j)**: `\usepackage{graphics/graphicx}` raw-loaded graphicx.sty over our graphics binding and the first `\rotatebox` exhausted memory (2309.12794); `source_tree_basename` takes the basename when the path resolves to the same file and the basename has a binding. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::a_package_by_its_installed_path_uses_its_binding`.
- **An xargs optional-argument command expanded outside typesetting (82c)**: xargs.sty:205-212 defines a `\newcommandx` command with any optional argument as `\@protected@testopt\cs…`/`\@protected@testopt@xargs\cs…` (:20-25), unexpanded whenever `\protect` is not `\@typeset@protect`; our binding built a plain macro and the gullet's `optional_arg_protected` saw only a leading `Optional` parameter, so `\sequence[2][2 = undefined]` (plain first argument) expanded inside ifthen's `\xdef` of the outer `\set`'s test and ran its own `\ifthenelse`: Fatal "File ended while scanning use of a Until:\NOT argument" (2002.09653). The first parameter is flagged `testopt` when any is optional and the gullet honours the flag. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::an_xargs_optional_command_is_protected_in_an_ifthen_test`.
- **A LaTeX picture inside PiCTeX kept PiCTeX's `\put` (82d)**: postpictex.tex:39-46 redefines `\@picture` to give a LaTeX picture LaTeX's `\put`, `\multiput`, `\frame` and `\linethickness` back; our `{picture}` (sect13.rs) bypasses `\@picture`, so 1301.4518's `\put{$\begin{picture}(50,50)\put(3,0){…}\end{picture}$} at 3.5 -1` read the inner `\put` as PiCTeX's, which scanned to the input's end for `at` (a pushback-limit Fatal; pdflatex 0). The environment makes those `\let`s when PiCTeX saved them (`\!latex@picture`). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::a_latex_picture_inside_pictex_keeps_latex_put`.
- **hyperref's autoref tag ran the document's `\null` (81b)**: `\lx@autorefnum@@` (hyperref_sty.rs), the autoref text every numbered target builds (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #418), ends with the token `\null` (the delimiter of the `\def\equationautorefname~#1\null{…}` idiom, DIVERGENCES #98); Perl's formatter ends with none. pdflatex runs `\null` only where an `\autoref` prints, so 1501.00090's `\def\null0{ \subsection{Other formats} … }` (hiding a subsection) ran its `\subsection` in every `\section`'s tag: Fatal Stomach:Recursion, no creators kept. The tag saves the document's `\null`, runs plain.tex's `\hbox{}` copy `\lx@autoref@null` and restores it by `\let` (no group: a name opening a `\bgroup` would meet it, 78j). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch81::an_authors_null_stays_out_of_the_autoref_tag` (repro cross-references/hyperref_autoref_tag_runs_the_kernel_null). Seen: that `\hbox{}` leaves an empty `<text xml:id="S1.1"/>` in every hyperref document's autoref tags (OPEN_ROWS frontmatter-adjacent singleton (h)).
- **A creator's fuzzy name label leaked a mark's comma (80v)**: `Tag ltx:creator after_close` (author_macros.rs) appends `fuzzy:<name text>` to the comma list `_annotations`, as Perl does (Base_Utility.pool.ltxml:873); a name showing `$^{a,b}$` left the bare label `b`, which our by-mark respelling in `relocate_annotations` (Rust-only) read as that name's own claim on the `$^b$` line, so a second name marked `b` lost it (0806.3762: Pujolas). The label is one label now (its commas `_`). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::revtex_marked_address_goes_by_mark`. The leak had masked three by-mark gaps, fixed with it (80y): a marked line's key was read from its tokens joined without the boundary after a control word (`$^{\rm a}$` → `\rma`, no key: `\lx@sup@setlabel@affiliation` reads the TeX spelling now, 2008.00493); spacing in or before a name's script was no mark (`{}^{a,\;b}`, `{}^{\rm b,\ c}`, `\,{}^{b,c}`: read from the XMath since 90a, #911, 1307.6166, hep-th0410034, 2311.14432); a bracketed number was none (`$^{[1]}$` against `$^1$`, 1803.06822). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::marked_list_marks_link_however_spelt`.
- **The FM writer left a lone suffixed id (80u)**: an item today's frontmatter places under several creators is numbered once per copy (`footnotex3`, `modify_id`'s `footnotex3a`); the writer taking the copy that held the plain id left the other's suffix standing alone (1606.04500, 1607.07280). `writer::apply::reclaim_ids` gives the taken ids back to the copy that stays. Guard `perfect_kernel_batchfms2b::footnote_marked_affiliations_go_to_their_names`.
- **cleveref `\crefname`/`\Crefname` derivation (77s)**: our clean port (`cref_define_name`, cleveref_sty.rs) dropped cleveref.sty:1296-1325's derivation of the other variant (Perl loads the raw macro), so a lone `\crefname{appendixtable}{Appendix Table}{…}` left `\Cref` with no name (2404.11739). Derived since 77s, changing case for both names or neither as the singular is empty or not (cleveref.sty:1309). Related Perl defect: KPE #998 (the `\label[type]` it names). Guards `perfect_kernel_batch77s::{cleveref_label_type_names_the_reference, cleveref_derived_name_follows_the_singular}`.
- **An unnumbered heading's title font (78a)**: our `\@@unnumbered@section` (sect04.rs) dropped Perl's `\lx@format@title@font@@` (latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:666-670), so a starred section lost its class font (IEEEtran's small caps, sectsty's and titlesec's formats; ≈61,800 IEEEtran papers with a `\section*`). Restored, with titlesec's after-code (OD #785) and the title braced as a numbered one's. Guards `perfect_kernel_batch78a::{ unnumbered_section_takes_the_title_font, titlesec_after_code_closes_an_unnumbered_title}`.
- **Text merged into a text node is ligated (78a)**: where libxml merged an insertion into the last text child (`open_text_internal`), the parent stayed current, so the run was never closed and never ligated — text after a formula `cleanup_math` turns into spaces (`500$\,$hPa 1989--2008`, nucl-ex0511052, 2208.08472). The merged node is current now, its run ligated from where it starts (Perl `setNode`, Document.pm openText_internal). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch78a::text_after_a_space_only_formula_is_ligatured`.
- **`\halign` inserts `\everycr`; AMS-TeX's active `@` (78b)**: the `\halign` primitive inserts `\everycr` after its preamble and after every row (tex.web §774/§799; Perl stores the register only, KPE #1019), and AMS-TeX's active `@` reads its connectors (KPE #1018) — lamstex `\newCD` diagrams, which count their rows in `\everycr`, are built (math0005042 28 errors → 0) where the `@` alone took them to Fatal; their arrowheads still print as lams font letters (the font-map row below). Guards `perfect_kernel_batch78b::*`.
- **thmtools' unnamed theorems (78k)**: our `\declaretheorem` (thmtools_sty.rs) dropped Perl's fallback name (thmtools.sty.ltxml:80-82 `\MakeUppercase` of the environment), so a theorem declared without `name=` was headed by its number alone ("1.2"; 1505.04294's 90 theorems, 70 census papers). Restored as thm-kv.sty:359 has it, `\thmt@modifycase` with the `uppercase`/`lowercase`/`anycase` options (OD #829). Guards `perfect_kernel_batch78k::{thmtools_unnamed_theorem_is_headed_by_its_environment, thmtools_unnamed_theorem_follows_the_case_option}`.
- **subfig's `\newsubfloat` was a no-op (78s)**: our subfig_sty.rs stubbed it (Perl subfig.sty.ltxml:30-62 makes the machinery), so a `\subfloat` in a float type other than figure and table — 2411.16612's `\newsubfloat[captionskip=10pt]{listing}` for minted's `listing` — found no `\lx@subfloat@<type>` and its sub-floats were lost. Made at run time since 78s (`define_subfloat`: the `sub<type>` counter, its macros and the `lx@subfloat@@<type>` environment through `EnvironmentBuilder` and a parsed replacement), as subfig.sty:244-266 only for a type without a `sub<type>` counter (else subfig's warning), joined to `\sf@counterlist`, its `[keys]` the `\captionsetup[sub<type>]` settings (78s r2). Guards `perfect_kernel_batch78s::{newsubfloat_of_a_float_type, newsubfloat_keys_and_an_existing_type}`.
- **A class copy with classic Mac line ends was one comment (79b)**: `shipped_class::class_code` split lines at line feeds only, so a copy saved with carriage returns (23 of the census's 164 sn-jnl.cls: 2609.04047, 2106.13602) was read as a single `%` comment and none of the code a binding looks for in it was found (`class_code_contains`, `class_text_loads`, `class_code_argument`): 2609.04047's appendix tables stayed "Table 17" where pdflatex, which ends a line at CR, LF or CR LF, prints "Table A1". Lines end at any of the three since 79b. No other copy-reading binding's census copies are CR-only. Guards `shipped_class::tests::class_code_ends_a_line_at_a_carriage_return`, `perfect_kernel_batch79b::snjnl_copy_with_carriage_returns_letters_its_appendix_floats`.
- **lamstex arrowheads print as letters** (PERL-ORIGIN, found by rev78b): a `\newCD` diagram's arrowheads and dotted shafts are `\char`s of the lams1-lams5 fonts (lamstex `\arrow@i\char119`, `\arrow@ii\char104`, `\arrow@iii\char13`), which no font map covers, so they print as "w", "h", "ﬂ" in the text font (math0005042's pullback diagram: cells `h * [   wu]`, `e _ 0 * [ﬂﬂﬂﬂﬂ ﬂıβ   pull back  w   p u]`). Open: lams1-lams5 font maps (arrowheads and shafts to Unicode arrows and rules) or drop the glyphs.
- **A pgfkeys key that expands to one brace group (79w)**: our native dispatch (pgfkeys_code_tex.rs `dispatch_item`) added the default path to the expanded key as it stood, where pgfkeys' `\pgfkeys@addpath#1\relax` (pgfkeys.code.tex:540-543) takes it as a delimited argument that loses one brace group: `\def\RelayedType{{node}}` … `\node[\RelayedType]` was "I do not know the key '/tikz/node'" (2110.08325's grids, 501 errors and the runaway Fatal; pdflatex 0). Perl runs the raw loop. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch79w::a_braced_key_in_a_macro_names_its_style`.
- **A package's own `\zap@space` around a load (79x)**: content.rs zapped the option list stored in `\opt@<file>` with the kernel's rule whatever `\zap@space` meant, where latex.ltx:18514-18517 expands `\zap@space#2 \@empty` with the current meaning: tikz-timing.sty:2331-2341 lets its own (`#1 \@empty{#1}`) around its library loads, so `\usetikztiminglibrary[rising arrows]{clockarrows}` stored "risingarrows", an unknown key (2409.18120; pdflatex 0). `zapped_options` expands the list when `\zap@space` is not the kernel's copy (`\lx@kernel@zap@space`, sect01.rs). Perl stores the list unzapped. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch79x::a_package_zap_space_keeps_the_option_spaces`.
- **An argument a template's cell brace closes (79y)**: 69c carries `{eqnarray}`'s and amsmath's cell braces (`$\displaystyle{##}$`) as `\lx@template@bgroup`/`\lx@template@egroup`, which a balanced read took for ordinary tokens; when a cell's `}` has closed the template's `{` and a macro argument's `{` holds the alignment count at 0, the `&` ends the cell mid-argument (tex.web §342) and the v-part's `}` must end the argument. gullet.rs `read_balanced_with_close` closes it at the egroup: `a^{b}_c} - \frac{x}{y_{d}&=& 0.` is `\frac{x}{y_{d}}` (0902.2869: 6 errors → 0, lines 409-1043 back in the document). The kernel `{tabular}` reading is an OPEN_ROWS residual. Guards `perfect_kernel_batch69c::{eqnarray_cell_braces_balance_across_the_template, amsmath_aligned_cell_braces_balance_across_the_template}`.
- **ctable's options after a line end (80d)**: our native `\ctable` (ctable_sty.rs; Perl has no binding and raw-loads ctable.sty) read its options with `OptionalKeyVals`, which stops at a space, where ctable.sty:206's `\newcommand{\ctable}[4][]` looks past it (`\@ifnextchar[`): `\begin{ctable}` + a line end + `[caption=…,label=…]` took `[` for the column spec ("Extra alignment tab", no caption, the label lost; 1706.01884: 35 errors and 12 dangling `\ref` → 0). `SkipSpaces` before the options since 80d. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::a_ctable_environment_reads_its_options_after_a_line_end`.
- **caption's `\captionbox` over a class's box (80i)**: our caption binding (Perl's has no `\captionbox`; it also stands in for caption3, which defines none) defined its `\captionbox` macro whatever was there, where caption.sty:454's `\newcommand*` keeps a defined one: ws-jmrr.cls:675's `\newbox\captionbox`, measured by its `\tbl`, became the macro under `subfig[caption=false]`/floatrow ("Expected a relational token … \begingroup" at each `\tbl`; 2303.09686: 2 errors → 0). Defined only where undefined since 80i. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::caption3_keeps_a_class_captionbox`.
- **nicematrix's `\tabularnote[label]` (80i)**: our binding passed the label, which its `OptionalBalanced` parameter reads without the brackets, as `\footnote#1`: `[x]` became the note's text and the text fell into the cell, and `[$\star$]` opened math in the note's argument that closed nowhere (2602.00629: 54 errors, every section after the table lost, 9 dangling `\ref` → 0/0, graphics 8 → 25). The label goes back inside brackets as `\footnote[label]`'s mark (nicematrix.sty:577/627) since 80i. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::nicematrix_tabularnote_label_kept`.
- **A binding's guessed theorem refused the document's `\newcommand` (80k)**: `\lx@guess@newtheorem` (neurips_sty.rs's and tac_cls.rs's `\AtBeginDocument` sets, OmniBus `theorem_preload`) yielded only to a later `\newtheorem` of the name; its commands blocked a `\newcommand`/`\newenvironment`, which in LaTeX defines a name the class never declared (neurips_2026.sty has no `{result}`): 2606.17122's body `\newcommand{\result}[2]{#1{\(\pm\,#2\)}}` was "Ignoring redefinition", every `\result{…}{…}` table cell opened a theorem (87 errors, Fatal → 0, 0 dangling). The guess runs under `DefinitionOrigin::Fallback` since 80k, so its meanings are fallback meanings (`state::is_fallback_meaning`). Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::neurips_guessed_theorem_yields_to_document_newcommand`.
- **The conditional guard's Fatal kept nothing (80k)**: `digest_step_guarded` (core_interface.rs) salvaged what was digested before a Fatal only for the Stomach and TooManyErrors targets, where Perl's `hardYankProcessing` keeps it for every Fatal (Common/Error.pm:336-338); `Timeout:IfLimit` wrote a 39-byte document. A finite figure-heavy paper reaches the 48M limit as surely as a loop: 2006.14937's 24 matplotlib `.pgf` figures (5.3 MB) trip it at the ninth, once 80j's `\pdfstrcmp` let its datatool preamble through (int98a's TooManyErrors salvage had kept 6,821 words). The guard's Fatal now keeps every level, as TooManyErrors does (a run of conditionals adds no boxes; with the loop in the body's own paragraph the innermost level is the whole body), and leaves the resource-fatal latch: kept, the document was still lost in the build, where the first formula the math parser could not read (`(a,b`) took the latched Fatal for its own (`take_last_resource_fatal`, parser.rs) and the converter wrote an empty file. The phases after digestion get the guard's budget afresh. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch80a::iflimit_fatal_keeps_the_document_before_it`.
- **hyperref's `\Hy@driver` was `hpdftex` whatever the output mode (101a)**: hyperref_sty.rs defined it as a constant, so a shipped siamart copy (siamart251216.cls:1302-1336 `\ifpdf\else\usepackage{hypdvips}\fi`) under the DVI persona (`\pdfoutput=0`, K6) met hypdvips.sty:27-36's "Wrong hyperref driver `hpdftex.def' selected!" Error — 14 papers of the cortex 2605/2606 reruns (2605.04427, 2605.04786, 2605.05043, 2605.08634, 2605.14933, 2605.26592, 2605.28291, 2605.17732, 2606.08053, 2606.13198, 2606.14937, 2606.15203, 2606.16251, 2606.22191, 2606.26900) and 1703.08345. It is now what hyperref.sty's `\Hy@AtEndOfPackage` (:4246-4407) settles on: `hpdftex` under `\ifpdf` whatever option (`hluatex` under the `luatex` profile), `hxetex` under XeTeX, else the last DVI driver option, the class's global ones read first (`dvipdfmx` → `hdvipdfm`, …), defaulting to `hdvips`; `\Hy@PutCatalog` (hypdvips.sty:3091-3108) is hpdftex.def:40's draft-mode `\@gobble` (Perl's binding defines neither). Under DVI, hrefhide.sty:153-166 now raises "Producing not a pdf file", as latex+dvips does. Residual: hyperref's wrong-driver-option Errors (`[dvips]` in PDF mode, `[pdftex]` in DVI mode, hyperref.sty:2528-2563) are not raised, and the `driverfallback` key (ctex's `driverfallback=dvipdfmx`, ctex-engine-pdftex.def:493) is not honoured; hypdvips.sty:2002 `\g@addto@macro{\endfigure}` is dropped with Perl's Warning (`\endfigure` a constructor, Package.pm:2542). Guards `perfect_kernel_batch101a::{siamart_copy_loads_hypdvips_in_dvi_mode, hyperref_driver_follows_the_output_mode}` (the latter's `[dvips]`-in-PDF and `[pdftex]`-in-DVI shapes pin the missing wrong-option Error, not TeX). Review rev101a: SHIP (driver matched latex/pdflatex/xelatex/lualatex on every probe; PDF-persona probes byte-identical to base). Also not modelled, no witness: `tex4ht` in DVI (`\Hy@texhttrue`, colorlinks), `customdriver`, the `\DocumentMetadata` `hgeneric-testphase` path (hyperref.sty:2738-2750), a user `\Hy@defaultdriver`. Under DVI hypdvips.sty now runs to its end and redefines `\begin`, `\end`, `\@sect`, `\bibitem`, `\@footnotetext`, `\contentsline` (base stopped at :31); the witnesses and a feature-rich probe came out byte-identical, but a later unlocking of those kernel macros brings them into play.
- **A recovered Fatal left the Stomach's token stack full (int131fix)**: `invoke_token` (stomach.rs) pops its `token_stack` entry only on a normal exit, and the recovery arms that keep the document (`core_interface.rs` `digest_step_guarded`'s Err arm, the streaming resource stop, `streaming_restart::release_before_rerun`) reset the box lists but not the stack, so after a `Stomach:Recursion` Fatal the build's first digestion (103c's `kind_legend_contacts` reading a contact name) failed its first push: a second Fatal, "build stopped early" (2410.07647: 96 of 886 words in the Bundle 1 A/B; 2105.02599 0 words on both sides). `stomach::clear_token_stack` in each arm, as Perl's `hardYankProcessing` (Common/Error.pm:329); WISDOM #101. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch131fix::a_recovered_fatal_leaves_no_token_stack_behind`.
- **The null control sequence's optional-argument internal was `\\` (int131fix)**: `define_optional_internal` (sect08.rs, batch 66b, OD #567) named the internal `"\\" + cs`, which for `\csname\endcsname` is `\\` itself, so `\expandafter\newcommand\expandafter{\csname\endcsname}[1][x]{…}` replaced the line break (2410.07647: changes' `\csname\Changes@commandname\endcsname`, its `\Changes@commandname` undefined in our binding; a `{#1\\}` body recursed to the base's Fatal). Named as `\string` prints it, `\\csname\endcsname` (latex.ltx:1252/1255, tex.web §263); Perl clean. Guard `perfect_kernel_batch131fix::null_cs_optional_internal_keeps_the_line_break`.

### From "Open items" — R4 detail

### R4 — `--preload=<cls>` trips the LaTeX hook stack (`Extra \PopDefaultHookLabel`)
- **Symptom:** `--preload=article.cls` prints `LaTeX hooks Error: Extra \PopDefaultHookLabel` (clean without preload or with `LATEXML_NODUMP=1`).
- **Mechanism:** `\@pushfilename` changes meaning mid-load: `article` is pushed before `LaTeX.pool` loads, using a pre-pool `\@pushfilename` that does not touch `\g__hook_name_stack_seq`. The pool installs expl3's `\@popfilename`, which pops an empty seq and errors.
- **Resolution:** A TeX-side repair or re-synchronizing the sequence at the point `LoadPool('LaTeX')` executes.

FX26/FX26b (binding declarations dropping the tail from the check's print) parked after two NO-SHIPs, each silencing a counted loss.

## Bundle int161a — QA45 list labels, QA54 internals, QA30 bibunits, the DTL1 box-size chain

On `pk/int161a` after the int160a push (`2d74850c2f`); every batch reviewed and merged.

- **ML1 (QA45, BM Q3.2):** `\list` keeps its label in `\@itemlabel`, read at each `\item` by the locked `\@trivlist` (latex.ltx:15858/15966); a class's own `\list` (memoir) labels as pdflatex; a copied `\trivlist` that clears the label, and a label redefined mid-list, as rows (OD #180).
- **PI1 (QA54 fix b):** amsmath's `\@@eqno`/`\@@leqno`/`\veqno`, leqno/reqno setting `\iftagsleft@` (1409.3102); lineno loads etoolbox and kvoptions, microtype keyval (KPE #1222). Its lineno change lets a document's `\let\ifpdf\relax` hold as in TeX: 1306.0471's 16 new `unexpected:fi` are pdflatex's (KPE #487; rows `a_disabled_ifpdf_errs_where_tex_does`).

- **Chain int161a (the one large A/B):** gates 8041/8041; A/B 28,354 → 86 changed: 46 better, 38 lateral, 2 load noise (2302.07562, 2505.20009 re-checked identical), 0 worse (CL161); E 45,813 → 45,788, F 172 → 172; stage2 1,434 ids, creators changed 0; cost ≤ +0.083 %; nets 136 worse 0; sweep #176 G1-G4 0, G5 58 (tikzpingus-doc, a 172 s manual, timed out under load — instructions −0.03 % vs int160c).

### R3 — Bibliography-absence campaign

- **Bibliography-absence campaign** (PR #444) — **291 recovered / 20 338 entries**.
- Remaining unblocked: **R3d tab-mark parameter scan vs cell read**
- State: **R3d next** (12 papers left, unblocks alignment macro `&` splits)
- Size: medium
- Detail: Open items §R3, [`RESIDUAL.md`](../parity/bib_absence_2026-07-29/RESIDUAL.md)

- Detail (from SYNC_STATUS "Open items"):
- **R3d — RE-MEASURED + CLOSED int26 (agent_reports/2026-10-18_r3d_remeasure.md):** of the 28 papers
  (`docs/parity/bib_absence_2026-07-29/residual_characterization.tsv.gz`, `\lx@begin@alignment` rows) 27 convert to the
  end on head with their bibliographies; the last, 2606.03447, was physics' unguarded `[…]` read (KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS
  #981), fixed in int26 (`phys_read_optional`). The mechanism below was wrong: TeX's `macro_call` does not disable tab
  marks (that is `read_toks`, tex.web §482); ltcmd's `\group_align_safe_begin:` (latex.ltx:2103-2110) does, so a global
  guard in `Parameters::read_arguments` would be unfaithful (settled dead end; the 5 regressions were correct).
- **(historical) R3d: Alignment Parameter Scan vs Cell Read Distinction (`suppressed_tab_marks`):**
  - *Symptom:* An unescaped `&` inside a delimiter-fenced macro argument splits the alignment row and truncates the document (and bibliography). 12 papers remain affected.
  - *Mechanism:* `tex.web` §394 `macro_call` suppresses tab marks while scanning parameters. `SuppressedTabMarks` in [`latexml_core/src/common/local_assignments.rs:194`](../../latexml_core/src/common/local_assignments.rs#L194) fixed `physics.sty`'s `\mqty` (14 papers), but applying it globally to `Parameters::read_arguments` regressed 5 tests (`cells_test`, `numprints_test`, `xytest_test`, `consort_flowchart_test`, `unit_tests_by_silviu_test`) because that path also reads alignment cell content.
  - *Action:* Distinguish macro parameter scanning from alignment cell reading in `Parameters::read_arguments` so tab marks inside `{...}` do not split outer cells.
- **R3b — RE-MEASURED + CLOSED 2026-10-18 (agent_reports/2026-10-18_r3b_rootcause.md):** the six "real `\cite`, empty
  bibliography" papers all keep their bibliographies on 67int27 — 2605.14990 53, 2606.05629 19, 2606.10056 26,
  2606.17491 30, 2606.00231 31 bibitems, each = pdflatex+bibtex's `.bbl`, 0 E/F (count them in the post-processed
  output: the core XML's `<bibliography files=…>` is a placeholder latexml_post fills from the `.bib`). 2605.29754
  comments out biblatex's `\printbibliography` and types its list by hand (kept); its `\cite`s render the keys with
  `Warning:expected:ids Missing Entry`, as Perl (SHARED, honest; biblatex labels without an `ltx:bibliography` would be
  a 1-paper surpass, not pursued). Side finding, open: tlp.cls `\maketitle` leaves a group open — a Rust-only
  `Warning:unexpected:\end{document} … inside a group at level 1` (2606.10056; content intact; suspect the locked
  `\maketitle` replaying tlp.cls:519-536 `\@ifnextchar [{\m@ketitleone}…` through `\lx@deposit@maketitle`; repro
  scratch_356a0c47/r3b/repro_tlp/a.tex).
- **R3g: amsrefs bare `\begin{biblist}` — FIXED (71a):** a `{biblist}` outside `{bibdiv}` opens its own bibliography
  through the backmatter route (amsrefs_sty.rs; KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #939).

- **ST3 + ST3b + ST3c:** state a conversion leaves for the next on one thread reset per conversion (`reset_conversion_state` at `initialize_report`): keyvals' report-once set, refstep's pending steps, the mouth-closed budget, the class-derivative stack, listings' counters.
- **RG1 (int162a's follow-up, 2410.06985):** a paragraph in a group opened mid-paragraph kept; a group's blank paragraph measures no line (OD #100). **DTL1d:** out-of-range numbers/dimensions/e-TeX overflow are Errors with TeX's value (OD #519). **PGR1b** (int163a's follow-up): pgfmath reads a `\csname`-built name as one token (pagelayout manuals 174 E → 0). **CAP1** (int163a's follow-up): caption lets `\@makecaption` to a shorthand-free wrapper (OD #1011; 11 asmeconf papers −2 E).
- **Chain int164a (the one large A/B):** gates 8056/8056; A/B 28,354 → 901 changed (777 frontmatter), E 45,784 → 46,288 (+504: DTL1d's promotion of pre-existing Rust-only overflow Warnings, XML byte-identical — 2401.12710 +299 varwidth = minipage, 2407.10017 +171 pgfplots; 1604.08500 right, TeX errs), F 172 → 172; CL164: 2 regressions → immediate follow-ups — PMK1 (P4b2b: a raw IEEEtran-derived class's robust `\IEEEauthorrefmark` replayed from its inner name prints the `\ifcase` table into names; 0912.0896, 2609.25589) and DTL1h (pgfmath's `16383.99999pt` = `\maxdimen` read as overflow; 1811.10950; and 2109.14286's `12319.0+4442`, the one worse net); 1103.1294 −1,134 words (already Fatal: the cap trips sooner); cost: P4b part 1's `leads_with_label` rehearses every REVTeX/AASTeX `\affiliation` — 2103.04797 +10.7 %, 1901.01540 +1.7 % (PERF164; P4b's own cost run had been skipped) → CST1; nets 136 worse 1 (2109.14286 → DTL1h); sweep #179: pagelayout's two manuals clean again (PGR1b), G1 3 — all DTL1d's promotion of pre-existing Rust-only overflows: ddphonism (40 E), tikzbrickfigurines-doc (352 E) the `±16383.99999pt` = `\maxdimen` literal (→ DTL1h), algxpar-doc (784 E) varwidth = minipage (→ VARW, before the merge for the G1 bar); G2-G4 0, G5 58.
